Over approximately ten months, her family and church friends observed that she became markedly less reliable—forgetting potlucks she’d organized, showing up to boxing and work in an unusually agitated, driven state, and reacting defensively when others questioned her decisions—leading to friction with coworkers and threats that she might lose her job. department, where psychiatric services were consulted.\n- Chief Complaint: “I’ve been feeling wound up and on edge for months, like my mood is stuck on high and I’m snapping at everyone, even my own family.”\n▸ Patient-specific Symptom Manifestations\n- S019 (Delusions): I know it sounds crazy to other people, but I’m sure the folks at the warehouse and even some people at church are watching me and trying to set me up to get fired, like they’re all in on some plan because I’m meant to do something big and they’re scared of that.\n- S020 (Hallucinations): Pretty much every day now, especially at night or when I’m alone in the break room, I hear these voices clear as day arguing about me, saying I’m a disappointment or that I need to ‘step up and save people,’ even when nobody’s around and there’s no radio on.\n- S021 (Disorganized_Thinking_Speech): When I try to explain what’s going on, my words just run together and I jump from talking about work to church to boxing to my husband, and people keep stopping me saying they can’t follow what I’m saying, even though in my head it all connects.\n- S022 (Disorganized_Abnormal_Behavior): Sometimes at work or at home I get so wound up and confused that I just pace back and forth for an hour, talking to myself, flapping my hands or making these weird little gestures, and if someone tells me to sit down or calm down I either ignore them or snap at them without really meaning to.\n- S023 (Negative_Symptoms): Other times, especially after one of those big blowups, I just shut down for days, barely talk to anyone, skip boxing and church, sit on the couch staring at the TV without really watching it, and even taking a shower or cooking feels like too much effort.\n- S033 (Elevated_Irritable_Mood): For months I’ve felt like I’m stuck on high voltage, bouncing between feeling super amped up and invincible to getting pissed off at the smallest thing, like my husband asking about the bills or a coworker moving my pallet, and I end up yelling or slamming stuff before I even realize I’m doing it.\n- S034 (Increased_Activity): It’s like I’ve got a motor inside me that won’t shut off—I’m cleaning the house at 3 a.m., reorganizing the stock room at work when I’m supposed to be loading, signing up for extra shifts and church events, and I can’t sit still long enough to finish any one thing.\n- S036 (Decreased_Sleep): Most nights I only sleep two or three hours, if that, because my mind is racing with ideas and plans, and instead of feeling tired I just get up and start doing things like writing lists, cleaning, or texting people in the middle of the night.\n- S039 (Manic_Distractibility): I’ll be in the middle of a serious talk with my boss or my husband and suddenly I’ll get pulled off by a buzzing light, a sound in the hallway, or a random thought about a new project, and next thing I know I’m off doing something else and never finished what I started.\n- S040 (Increased_Goal_Activity): I’ve been signing up for extra overtime, trying to start little side hustles, planning new church groups, and even pushing my husband to have sex way more than usual all at the same time, like I have to chase every idea right then or I’ll explode.\n- S041 (Painful_Consequence_Behavior): I’ve blown a bunch of money we don’t really have on boxing gear, church donations, and stuff online, driven way too fast when I’m upset, and met up with a couple guys from the gym behind my husband’s back because in the moment it all felt exciting and right and I didn’t really care what might happen after.\n▸ Additional Diagnostic Requirements\n- psychotic_symptoms and manic_episode occur concurrently: Back when I first started feeling super amped up and barely sleeping, that was also when the voices really kicked in and I became convinced people at work and church were plotting against me, all happening at the same time for months.\n- manic_episode is present for the majority of the total duration of the active and residual portions of the illness: For most of this whole stretch I’ve felt revved up, overactive, and wired, with only short spells where I crash and go quiet before I ramp right back up again.\n- delusions_and_hallucinations occur for 2 or more weeks in the absence of a major mood episode: There was a period a couple months ago when my mood felt more flat and drained than amped up, but the voices and the feeling that everyone was watching and testing me never let up during those weeks.\n- functional_impairment: Because of all this, I’ve been written up at the warehouse for not following instructions and leaving tasks half-done, my husband has talked seriously about separating, and I’ve stopped showing up reliably to church and boxing even though those used to be my main supports.\n- duration: Looking back, this whole thing has been building and messing with my life for close to a year now, pretty much since the money fights with my husband really started.\n- psychotic_symptoms_duration: I’ve been hearing the voices and feeling like people are out to get me for most of this time, definitely going strong for at least the last eight months.\n- manic_episode_duration: Feeling wired, barely sleeping, and constantly on the go has been happening for around half a year now, ever since I first started grabbing all those extra shifts at the lists, cleaning, or texting people in the middle of the night.\n- S039 (Manic_Distractibility): I’ll be in the middle of a serious talk with my boss or my husband and suddenly I’ll get pulled off by a
